### 1. Introduction

1.1 Business Purpose

The business purpose of this document is to provide a generic interface to business partners

who want to integrate with online remittances services of UniTeller. The business partners

could be a financial Web Portal or a financial service provider through Mobile Applications etc.

This interface allows the business partners to seamlessly communicate with the UniTeller

server by providing access to the UNIR API (Online Remittance API of UniTeller). For the scope

of this document the acronym UNIR stands for "UniTeller Internet Remittances".

1.2 Purpose

The purpose of this document is to introduce online remittances Application Programming

Interface (API). API makes all the methods available for integration along with their request and

response. This is a business as well as a technical document, and in conjunction with the UNIR

API Reference, a complete specification. It should allow any partner with the requisite

programming knowledge to adapt its systems to seamlessly integrate into UNIR.

1.3 Implementation Overview

When a business partner integrates with UNIR they need to have secure access to the UNIR API.

This integration is achieved by either creating a site-to-site VPN (Virtual Private Network) for

making API method calls over the VPN; or having HTTPS (Hypertext Transfer Protocol Secure)

restricted access to the UNIR servers. Over one of these secure connections UniTeller will

provide access to the testing/integration servers for the partner. Once the technical

implementation is complete by the partner, a set of certification tests will be performed and on

successful completion, production deployment will be done on both sides.

### 2 Overview of communication of with Online Remittances API

17

There are two possible ways to communicate with UNIR, either partner can have its own server

which will communicate with UNIR server (partner can have their own front end e.g. Mobile

App, web portal, IVR or call center) or partner can have just front end and directly

communicating with UNIR server (partner can have their own Mobile App or web portal).

Making an API Call

This section explains how to make the Online Remittances API call. It provides an overview of

the formats and standards you can use with the UNIR API.

2.1 Restful web services

This API is based on Restful web services. The Restful web services are based on HTTP methods

and the concept of REST. Representational State Transfer (REST) is a key design idiom that

embraces a stateless client-server architecture in which the web services are viewed as

resources and can be identified by their URLs. Web service clients that want to use these

resources access a particular representation by transferring application content using a small

globally defined set of remote methods that describe the action to be performed on the

resource. REST is an analytical description of the existing web architecture, and thus the

interplay between the style and the underlying HTTP protocol appears seamless.

2.2 Supported Request and Response Formats

This API supports JSON request and response formats that can be submitted using HTTP POST.

Here's an example of a JSON request that can be submitted via HTTP POST:

{

- employee": {
- name": "John",
- age": 30,
- city": "New York",
- cars": ["Ford", "BMW", "Fiat"]
}

}

18

### 3 Call Structure

Each Remittances API call consists of the following elements:

3.1 Service Endpoint

Remittances API requests are sent to the server endpoint. The complete path to a resource is

based on the below sample base URL.

[https://<domain](https://<domain)>/UTLROnlineRemitAPI/<API Type>/<API Resource name>

Sample URL

**Production:** [https://send.uniteller.com](https://send.uniteller.com)

**Test Server**: sendsandbox.uniteller.org

Example:

[https://sendsandbox.uniteller.org/UTLROnlineRemitAPI/Common/LicensedStates](https://sendsandbox.uniteller.org/UTLROnlineRemitAPI/Common/LicensedStates)

3.2 Security in Restful Web Services

This API is based on HTTP methods, so the following two types of security are implemented.

_3.2.1_ HTTPS Access

API is accessible over HTTPS only to make the secure end to end communication, which means

all the requests and responses are being done over HTTPS.

_3.2.2_ OAuth2.0

The OAuth 2.0 authorization framework is a protocol that allows a user to grant a third-party website or application access to the user's protected resources, without necessarily revealing

their long-term credentials or even their identity.

Types of Grant Type:

**Client Credentials:** Using this grant type, an API partner can access the Online RemittanceSystem _API’s which doesn’t deal with user’s read_-write information ( E.g.: CountryStates ,

Signup).

19

**Password:** Using Password grant type, a partner can access the Online Remittance System _API’swhich deals with user’s read_-write information (E.g.: AddBeneficiary, EditBeneficiary,

SendMoneyPreview).

**Refresh token:** Using this grant type, one can refresh the expired Access token through refreshtoken, that we got in the response of Client Credentials or Password grant flow. Refresh tokens

have single use only so cannot be reused, and when they are used they also invalidate the token they are associated with.

NOTE: _APIs which don't deal with user’s read_-write information can be accessed using bothClient Credentials and Password grant type.

JWT(JSON Web Token):

**Access Token:** This _JWT token contains authorities encoded inside its payload so we don’t need_to create session and store authorization , rather we are using a stateless session policy.

**Refresh Token:** In the case of expiration of access token, refresh token is used to get a newaccess token. To get an access token using refresh token a request is sent to the _authorization_

**server** with a refresh token, authorization grant type (i.e. refresh_token grant type) and PartnerSecret Key. In response, a new JWT access token and a refresh token with new validity are

returned.

How to Generate the JWT Tokens:

To get an access token and refresh token a request is sent to the authorization server with the Partner Secret key and authorization grant type (i.e. Client Credentials /password). In response,

20

a JWT access token and a JWT refresh token is returned. An access token has less validity than a refresh token.

URI: [https://<domain](https://<domain)>/oauth/token

HTTP Method: POST

_E.g.:_ [https://sendsandbox.uniteller.org/oauth/token](https://sendsandbox.uniteller.org/oauth/token)

REQUEST HEADER:

| Key | Value |
| --- | --- |
| Authorization | Basic < Partner Secret Key > |
| application_version | 1.0 |
| PLATFORM | IOS |
| X - Forwarded - For | User’s IP Address |

**Authorization:** Partner Secret Key is confidential so, it will be provided through an Email by theUniTeller Team and it should be stored in a secured way on the Partner Side.

**Application version:** Providing an Application version in each header request is not mandatorybut is recommended. We can have some server-side control on the application and can be used

if required.

**PLATFORM:** Platform name is used to identify the request device platform. We use thefollowing values: IOS, WEB, ANDROID.

**X-Forwarded-For:** _User’s IP Ad_dress should be passed.

**NOTE:** Header remains the same for all the API’s only Request Body differs**depending upon the grant type.**

CLIENT CREDENTIALS GRANT:

REQUEST BODY:

The Partner requests the token endpoint by adding the following parameters using the

- application/x-www-form-urlencoded".
The grant_type is REQUIRED and its value MUST be set to "client_credentials".

Key Value

21

**grant_type** client_credentials

RESPONSE:

| Attribute | Description | Type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Access_token | JWT token generated based upon the grant type | JWT |
| Token_type | Type of JWT token(E.g. Bearer) | String |
| Expires_in | The Expiry Time in which the Token will expire(in Seconds) | Integer |
| Scope | The scope of the client(read) | String |

MFA GRANT:

The Partner requests the token endpoint by adding the following parameters using the

- application/x-www-form-urlencoded".
grant_type REQUIRED. The value MUST be set to "mfa".

mfa_token REQUIRED. The refresh token was HTTP issued to the partner.

mfa_code REQUIRED. The Value received on the Register device(Mobile).

Is_trusted REQUIRED. The value must be YES(Save the Device) or NO(Don’t want to save the

device)

REQUEST:

| Key | Value |
| --- | --- |
| grant_type mfa_token | Mfa <MFA Token > |
| mfa_code is_trusted | <MFA Code> <YES or NO> |

RESPONSE:

| Attribute access_token | Description JWT token generated based | Type JWT |
| --- | --- | --- |
| token_type | upon the grant type Type of JWT Token | String |
| refresh_token | This token is used when JWT accessToken is expired to | JWT |

generate new Access and Refresh Token

**expires_in** The Expiry Time in which the IntegerToken will expire(in Seconds)

**Scope** Scope of the client String

26

| responseCode | Response Code | String |
| --- | --- | --- |
| responseMessage | Response Message | String |

REFRESH TOKEN GRANT:

If the authorization server issued a refresh token to the client, the client makes a refresh request

to the token endpoint by adding the following parameters using the "application/x-www-form-

urlencoded".

grant_type REQUIRED. The value MUST be set to "refresh_token".

refresh_token REQUIRED. The refresh token was HTTP issued to the partner.

REQUEST:

| Key | Value |
| --- | --- |
| grant_type refresh_token | refresh_token <Refresh Token > |

RESPONSE:

| Attribute access_token | Description JWT token generated based | Type JWT |
| --- | --- | --- |
| token_type | upon the grant type Type of JWT Token | String |
| refresh_token | This token is used when JWT accessToken is expired to | JWT |

generate new Access and Refresh Token

**expires_in** The Expiry Time in which the IntegerToken will expire(in Seconds)

28

| Scope | Scope of the client | String |
| --- | --- | --- |
| responseCode | Response Code | String |
| responseMessage | Response Message | String |

### 4 Let’s Start API Integration

This section explains the way to introduce the **UNIR API into a partner’s web or mobile**

application. This workflow just provides a high level review of the process/features of the

remittances services. There could be other workflows possible for various use cases

requirements of the partners._._

In order to send money, user has to go through several processes, as mentioned below-

| ● | Register user: User has to register in the UniTeller system. |
| --- | --- |
| ● | Setup profile: Once User has logged in, they need to setup their profile. This step has |

some sub steps, which is exp_lained below in the “_Setup profile_” section._

32

- **●** _Add Beneficiary:_ After successful completion of setup profile, user’s can add
beneficiaries to transfer the funds.

- **●** _Add Sending Methods:_ In order to send money, users need to add sending methods to
their profile to make payments.

- **●** _Send Money:_ After completing all the above steps, the user is ready to send money to
their added beneficiaries. Send Money itself has some sub steps, explained below in the “Send

Money” section.

Users will have Edit Profile, Edit Beneficiaries, Edit Sending Methods, Deactivate Beneficiaries,

Deactivate Sending Methods etc. capabilities also.

Here are the use cases of different modules mentioned above-

3.

4.1 Register user

_In order to register in the UniT_eller System, user’s have to provide some basic information. This

includes the user’s name, email address, date of birth and his/her preferred destination country

etc. Registration has following steps-

1) _User provides Email address (_after registration Email will be used as user ID and will be
**used to send email to the user**), Name, date of birth and his/her preferred destination

country.

**2)** System checks if a user already exists in the UNIR system in any status. The UNIR system has
two rules to check existing users, if user ID (Email Address) exists or combination _of user’s first_

name, last name and date of birth exists. Then the UNIR system assumes that the user is already

registered and takes action based on the user’s status etc.

If a user is registered with the UNIR system. The UNIR System does not allow users to create

another account.

**3)** If a user does not exist in the UNIR system, then it is registered in the UNIR system and an
authentication code is sent to the user’s registered email address.

4.2 Setup profile

33

Once a user is registered and logged in, he/she needs to set up a profile to be able to add a

sending method and send money. This module has following steps-

_1)_ **Complete personal info**: First step of setup profile is submitting user’s personal
information.

**2)** After successful submission of user’s personal information, information verification API
is called. Here are possible scenarios-

2.1) User may pass information verification and the user becomes **APPROVED** _(an_

**APPROVED** user can add sending methods and send money to his/her beneficiaries).

Click here for detailed technical flow

4.3 Add Beneficiary

After a successful login, a user can add beneficiaries/recipients. This is the process of

associating a beneficiary/recipient with a payer. It is important to mention that a PAYER can

have additional fields as well as a payer branch. Therefore for adding a beneficiary, API Partner

should first call /UTLROnlineRemitAPI/Common/v2/PayerWithReceptionMethodsList

The API PayerWithReceptionMethodsList will return an object of payerWithReceptionMethods.

Apart from other details of a payer, payerWithReceptionMethods object hold two more

important fields - isPayerBranchRequired [possible values: YES or NO ] &

isAddtionalFieldRequired [possible values : YES or NO ].

Based on configuration, Payer can have

**** Payer Branch and Additional Fields (call both API PayerBranches &PayerAdditionalField )

**** Either Payer Branch or Additional Fields (call either API PayerBranches &PayerAdditionalField )

**** Neither Payer Branch nor Additional Fields (No need to call API PayerBranch andPayerAdditionalField )

These information can be retrieved from below two APIs

| 1. | /UTLROnlineRemitAPI/Common/PayerBranches |
| --- | --- |
| 1. | /UTLROnlineRemitAPI/Common/ v2 /Pa yerAdditionalField |

For a better understanding of the flow, the below flow diagram can also be referred to.

34

Add Beneficiary Flow Chart

Please note that the maximum allowed number of beneficiaries/recipients for a Remitter is 6.

But this should not be treated as a static value. This number is configurable in the UNIR system

and in the future, this can change based on business requirements.

_1)_ If Payer Branch is not required or Payer Branch is provided in Add Beneficiary’s request,
then new beneficiary will be added and success response (response code **00000000**) will be

returned.

4.4 Add Sending Methods

To make a payment while sending money, the user needs to add at least one Sending Method. If

the user’s information verification is already done and the user’s profile is in APPROVED status,

then only a sending method can be added. Here are the steps to add sending method:

| 1) | Sending method’s (Card/Ban k Account) details collected. |
| --- | --- |
| 2) | Validation is done. |

35

**3)** After successful validation, UNIR System checks Sending Method Limit and if limit is
exceeded then, UNIR System does not allow adding more sending methods.

**4)** If the user is allowed to add more sending methods, the UNIR system checks the
sending method type.

**5)** If Sending method is Credit Card, then-
5.1. The UNIR system checks Credit Card Limits. If the limit is exceeded, then the Credit Card

will not be added to the user's profile.

**5.2. System also checks if the** Credit Card is already added to the user’s profile or not, if

already added then, it does not allow adding the Credit Card again, else Credit Card will be

added.

**6)** If Sending method is Debit Card, then-
6.1. The UNIR system checks Debit Card Limits. If the limit is exceeded, then the Debit Card

will not be added to the user’s profile.

_6.2._ System also checks if the Debit Card is already added to the user’s profile or not, if

already added then, it does not allow adding the Debit Card again, else the Debit Card will be

added.

For Test Environment the below Test details should be used_:_

36

Click here for more detailed technical flow

4.5 Send Money

If a user has added beneficiaries, he/she can send money to his/her registered beneficiaries.

The Send Money process consists of two steps Send Money Preview and Send Money Confirm,

described in the API reference section.

Note: In the UNIR system, a payer may have a payer-limit which refers to the amount of money

payer can accept. If a user is sending more than the payer-limit then the UNIR system could not

process the request. This check is applied on SendMoneyPreview API.

For example, if a user is sending $501 to a payer that has a limit of $500, the system will return a

response code and it should be handled by an API partner. Below is the Response from

SendMoneyPreview API refers to the same scenario.

{

- responseCode": "59903002",
- responseMessage": "The amount you are sending exceeds the allowed amount of $500 for the
selected payer. Please change the amount or select another payer."

},

User Transaction Limit based on User level:

LEVEL_NAME DAILY_LIMIT WEEKLY_LIMIT

| SILVER | 1999 | 3999 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| GOLD | 2999 | 4999 |
| PLATINUM | 4999 | 8999 |
| DIAMOND | 9999 | 12999 |

Number of days hold on transaction based on User level (Configurable)_:_

37

**TxSMSAlert:** In order to send the Sms and Email to the Beneficiary TxSmsAlert API should becalled based upon sendMoneyConfirm response (isOptedInSMSAlert:YES).

4.6 Cancel Transaction

The UNIR system allows only PAYABLE transactions to be cancelled.

In UNIR System Transaction can be cancelled by:

| ● | Remitter |
| --- | --- |
| ● | UNIR System |

The status of the transaction in the UNIR will be updated based on settlement time. If the

REFUND to the customer is given before the settlement time, then status will be treated as VOID

otherwise it will be considered as REFUND.

This is to note that VOID transaction status will be displayed as **CANCELLED** to the Remitter

inside the application.

4.1. Promotion

Online Remittance service provides the promotion future to promote the remittance business.

Use the promotion

1. Auto Apply Promotion. 2. Manual Apply Promotion
**Auto Apply Promotion:** Auto promotion will apply via the system based on promotionconfiguration. Auto applied promotion information will get on sendMoneyPreview response.

You will get promotion name, promotion discount and other promotion information.

**Manual Promotion:** Manual promotion will apply only when user provide the promotioncode during the send money.

Promotion Value

Uniteller support three type of promotion-

_1._ **Discount on service fee:** _Discount on service fee promotion applies only to the_service fee amount. The User’s can get the discount from 0.1 to 100%.
38

**Example:** it is a dummy value to explain the promotion.

Sending Amount Service fee Promotion Service Fee after promotion

100$ 3$ 100% service 0$ fee discount(PROM)

_2._ **Absolute promotion:** Absolute promotion will apply on service fee. Service feediscount applies up to absolute service fee.
Sending Amount Service fee Promotion Service Fee after promotion

| 100$ | 3$ | 2$ Absolute 1$ |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1000$ | 25$ | 6$ Absolute 19$ |

_Gift card._ **The** User’s will get a Gift card coupon like Amazon gift card.

Please refer the flowchart.

4.2. Bill Payment

This service is used to make payments for utility services, to the required institutions or utility

providers against bills issued by them.

Please refer to the below flow chart for the Bill payment API calls.

39

### 5 API Reference

This section describes the complete protocol for system integration with UNIR, irrespective of

the implementation type. It provides the details of various methods available in the API, which

are for universal use in any implementation.

This API divides the methods module wise so that they can be easily understandable. Method

definition explains about each and every method along with their description. It includes

method name, request & response. Parameters of the request and response are defined in the

API Reference docs.

40

Note:-

There are some instructions must follow the in all request:

- **●** Request Header Information
**o** Must provide the version number so we can easily identify the request app

version.

- **●** Request Body Information_._
**o** Mobile number/phone number should be without ISD code.

5.1 CheckDuplicateUser (Requires Client-Credentials Grant)

**Description**: CheckDuplicateUser API verifies whether the details provided by the user are

Unique in Uniteller's system, generally this api is used before calling sign up to verify whether

the email or the combination of First Name, Last Name & Date of Birth is unique.

**Note:*** Suppose “ABC” is a Partner who has some function and owns a database and stores the

user’s personal information. In this case a partner can store the flag signal to avoid calls again

and again during CheckDuplicateUser and Signup API.

**URI:** /UTLROnlineRemitAPI/Security/CheckDuplicateUser

_Possible Response codes return:_ [00000000, 59906002, 59906005, 59903005, 50103003,

59903001, and 50107007] (See Appendix C for detail of Response codes)

**UNIR Request:** VerifyUserRequest

Validation Attribute Description Type Rule

**partnerCode** Mandatory This information is provided by String(2-20UniTeller and it should be exactly Characters)

the same way as provided.

**interactionId** Optional This information is used to track Stringthe requests and responses. The

interaction ID received in request will be returned in response.

**extraFields** Optional Extra fields for future use. Array of ExtraField.See Appendix A

**Locale** Optional. Valid Locale. If no input is provided on Stringvalues are EN request, by default it would be

(English) or English. ES (Spanish).

41

**firstName** Conditional Remitter First Name. String(1-30(Required if Characters)

emailAdress is Empty.)

**lastName** Conditional Remitter Last Name String(2-30(Required if Characters)

emailAdress is Empty.)

**birthDate** Conditional Remitter Date of Birth String(8 Digits)(Requried if

emailAdress is Empty.)

**emailAddress** Conditional Remitter Email id String(5-50Characters)

**UNIR Response:** VerifyUserResponse

Attribute Description Type

**requestTimestamp** Time (EST) when request is received at UniTeller String

**responseTimestamp** Time (EST) when response is sent to Client from StringUniTeller

**executionTimestamp** Total time taken in processing of request at LongUniTeller

**interactionId** This information is used to track requests and Stringresponses. The interaction ID received in request

and same is being returned in response.

| responseCode | Returns Response Code. | String |
| --- | --- | --- |
| responseMessage | Returns Response Message. | String |

can use almost all the features of the application except send money and add a sending method

until they completes anyd verifies their personal information.

5.4 VerifyEmail(Requires Client Credential Grant)

**Description:** This API is used to verify the Email Address by providing the generated email

verification token in the request.

**Note:** The **request parameters** should be passed in the **request params** and not in the request

body

**URI(GET):** /oauth/verifyEmail

**Example**:oauth/verifyEmail?verificationToken=93439708&username=alohani303@mailinator.com

**Possible Response Code:** [00000000, 19906002, 19903009] (See Appendix C for Response

Code details.)

registered Email Address

5.8 InformationVerification (Requires Password Grant)

**Description:** Information verification is one step of profile completion. On the basis of provided

information, UniTeller verifies his/her identification and returns response code as SUCCESS and

FAIL. If the response code returns SUCCESS, it means information verification is completed and

If FAIL then the user can not be allowed in the UniTeller system.

5.9 Add Beneficiary (Requires Password Grant)

information, remittance currency, selected payer and receipt method etc. are passed as the

input.

additional field depending upon the Wallet configuration(which can be checked in

payerAdditionalField API) in AddBeneficiary API request. For Wallet Reception Method all the

field are mandatory which are required for Account Credit except account Number.

Credit Card or Debit Card then, Card number, card type, cvv, billing address etc. are passed as

75

input. After successful addition, for credit card or debit card two small amounts credit to the

card.

50108004, 50105003, 59903003, 51104001, 59903006, 59908003, 59908001, 59908002,

used to track requests and responses. The

interaction ID received in request will be

returned in response.**extraFields** Optional Extra fields for future Array of

use. ExtraField. See Appendix A

(English) or ES by default it would be (Spanish). English

are- be added in _user’s_Credit Card or profile.

Debit Card**Nickname** Optional. Nick name of added String(4-60

bank account sending Characters) method.

If not provided System will generate it.

76

**cardType** Conditional Card type. String(4-60This field must Characters)

be filled if the Sending

Method name is Credit Card

or Debit Card. Possible values

are [Visa, Master Card]

**cardNumber** Conditional Debit or Credit Card String(16 Digits)This field must number

be filled if the Sending

Method name is Credit Card

or Debit Card.

**expireDate** Conditional String(6 Digits)This field must

be filled if the Sending

Method name is Credit Card

or Debit Card. Valid format is

MMYYYY (i.e. month, year)

with no separator

between year, month. The

Expiration date must be [6]

digit long and it would

contain digit only.

**cvvNumber** Conditional Remitter's String(3-4It must be [3-4] credit/debit card cvv Characters)

long. number. This field must be filled if the

Card.**billingAddress** Conditional Remitter's Object of address

name is Credit Card or Debit Card.

5.24 SendMoneyPreview (Requires Password Grant)

**Description:** This method creates the remittance transaction for preview of the user. It checks

user limits, checks restrictions, calculates fee and exchange rate etc and present to the user as a

preview of a receipt. Only the transaction number is not shown at this point.

**Note**: For payers (like Payer Exito) that require the amount to be entered in local currency, the

regex for validating the amount can be retrieved from the /PayerWithReceptionMethodList API

response. When the amount is expected in local currency, the following fields are also required:

paymentAmount and selectedCurrencyIsoCode (must be set to the local currency)

_URI:_ /UTLROnlineRemitAPI/Remittance/SendMoneyPreview

_Possible Response Code:_ [00000000,59903005, 59906002, 50103003,50108004, 59903001,

50105002, 59903002, 59903007, 51102903,50105003, 59903003, 51107002, 51104002,

51104003, 51104004, 51107001, 51102904, 50105004, 51108002, 51104001, 59905003,

59905004, 59906004, 19906005, 19906002, 59906008] (See Appendix C for Response Code

details.)

5.25 SendMoneyConfirm (Requires Password Grant)

**Description:** This method creates the remittance transaction for payment to the beneficiary. It

does payment processing, creates a transaction number and email receipt to the user. If any

promotion coupon is applied on transaction successfully then must be provide promotion

coupon in confirmation transaction request otherwise transaction would be treated without

promotion.

_URI:_ /UTLROnlineRemitAPI/Remittance/SendMoneyConfirm

_Possible Response Code:_ [00000000, 59906002, 59903005, 50103003, 59903001, 50108004,

50105002, 59904001, 59906005, 51103002, 50102010, 51102001, 51108001, 59906005,

51102002 and 59906007] (See Appendix C for Response Code details.)

request will be returned in response.

5.26 CancelTransaction (Requires Password Grant)

**Description:** This method cancels an existing unpaid (Payable) remittance transaction in UNIR.

In certain cases cancel might be asynchronous, in which case requests will be received and

processed later after some time.

**URI:** /UTLROnlineRemitAPI/Remittance/CancelTransaction

_Possible Response Code:_ [00000000, 59903005, 59906002, 50103003, 59903001, 50108004,

50105002, 51103001, 51104010, 51104011, 51102005, 51104012, 59906005, 51102004,

51104013] (See Appendix C for Response Code details.)

5.27 TransactionSummary (Requires Password Grant)

**Description**: This method fetches the list of transactions, based on various criteria as inputs.

These criteria could be time period, user id and transaction number etc. Transaction list

returned contains transaction objects with some limited fields.

110

track requests and responses. The interaction ID received in

request will be returned in response.

| userId | (Spanish). Mandatory. | User ID. Registered with String(5 - 50 |

unique number which gets at Characters) the time of confirm

**transactionStatus** Optional The transaction status could Stringbe paid, cancel, hold, payable,

refund, replaced for searching transactions based on status.

**thisWeek** Optional The ThisWeek request Stringparameter is used for getting

current week transactions and it should be ThisWeek as

string in request.**lastWeek** Optional The LastWeek request String

parameter is used for getting previous week transactions

and it should be LastWeek as string in request.

**thisMonth** Optional The ThisMonth request Stringparameter is used for getting

current month transactions and it should be ThisMonth as

parameter use for getting

previous month transactions and it should be LastMonth as

be used with string format MMDDYYYY (i.e. toDate) month, day and year) with no

separator between year, month, day.

**beneficiaryNickName** Optional. The beneficiary nickname is String(1-100the name where the user saves Characters)

beneficiary details with nickname at the time of adding

beneficiary.**maxTransactionCount** Mandatory The maximum transaction Integer

count is the number of transactions and it is 5 by

default and provides the latest 5 transactions from the last 6

month.

6.4 Common API (Requires Client-Credentials or Password Grant)

Common API provides different categories (List of countries, List of country's states, list of

country's currencies, list of Payers, list of reception method) of methods which are required

during the process of Sign up and during send money after login.

_6.4.1_ LicensedStates

and returns the list of states. Licensed states are those states where UniTeller has license to

provide the remittance services

_Possible Response Code:_ [00000000, 59906002, 59903005, 50103003 and 59903001]

(See Appendix C for Response Code details.)

responses. The interaction ID received

in request will be returned in response.

**Description:** This API will provide the Payer with Reception Methods List based on Country

Currency and State ISO code. (There are states in different countries that need payer branch

information depending upon the selection of the state related to that specific county).

_Possible Response Code:_ [00000000, 59906002, 59903005, 50103003, 59903001, 59903006

and 59903007] (See Appendix C for Response Code details.)

track requests and responses. The interaction ID

received in request will be returned in response.

Country ISO Code would be destination country ISO code.

code. Currency ISO code would be destination

specific unique ISO code. State ISO code would be

_6.4.8_ SendingMethodsName

**Description:** This method provides a list of sending methods name which is used by remitter to

pay UniTeller and these could be Credit Card and Debit Card etc.

URI: /UTLROnlineRemitAPI/Common/SendingMethodsName

**Possible Response Code:** [00000000, 59906002, 59903005, 50103003 and 59903001] (See

Appendix C for Response Code details.)

_UNIR Request:_ SendingMethodsNameListReq

**Attribute Validation Rule Description TypepartnerCode** Mandatory This information is provided by String(2-20

and it should be exactly the same Characters) as provided.

**interactionId** Optional This information is used to track Stringrequests and responses. The

interaction ID received in request will be returned in response.

**extraFields** Optional Extra fields for future use. Array of ExtraField.See Appendix A

**locale** Optional. Valid Locale. If no input is provided in Stringvalues are EN request, by default it would be

(English) or ES English (Spanish).

**UNIR Response:** SendingMethodsNameListResp

Attribute Description Type

**requestTimestamp** Time (EST) when request is Stringreceived at UniTeller

| responseTimestamp | Time (EST) when response is sent to Client from UniTeller | String |
| --- | --- | --- |
| executionTimestamp | Total time taken in processing of request at UniTeller | Long |

**interactionId** This information is used to track Stringrequests and responses. The

186

interaction ID received in request and same is being returned in

| responseCode | response. Returns Response Code | String |
| --- | --- | --- |
| responseMessage extraFields | Return Response Message Extra fields for future use. | String Array of ExtraField. See |

Appendix A**sendingMethodsName** The sending methods name. Array of String

_6.4.11_ getPayerAdditionalFields

**Description:** Gets PayerAdditionalFields from outside the application without login. This will

return payer additional fields alongwith their options value as well as type of additional field

(text, dropdown,date). Based on payer configuration in the UNIR system, these additional fields

will be required at the time of adding beneficiary. To know if the additional fields belong to

beneficiary, please refer to the “fieldBelongsTo” attribute of JSON response of

PayerAdditionalField.

The types of additionalField for a payer in the UNIR system are:

| 1. | Text |
| --- | --- |
| 2. | Date(MM/DD/YYYY) |
| 3. | Dropdown |

URI: /UTLROnlineRemitAPI/Common/PayerAdditionalField

track requests and responses. The interaction ID received in

request will be returned in response.

**extraFields** Optional Extra fields for future use. Array of ExtraField.See Appendix A

**Locale** Optional. Locale. If no input is provided in StringValid request, by default it would be

values are English EN

(English) or ES

(Spanish).**currencyISOCode** Mandatory. The destination currency ISO String(3

It must be code. Characters) [3]

characters long.

must be [2- 30]

Pickup or WALLET

as requested.

_6.4.12_ Transaction Search (Requires Password Grant)

**Description:** This API is used to fetch the transactions status based on the requested filters .The

partners have to provide their Admin Users in order to get the access to this API.

NOTE:

- **●** _Accepted values of Status are:_ HOLD, PAID, REFUND, PAYABLE, CANCEL andCANCELPENDING_._
| ● | Status should be case sensitive . |
| --- | --- |
| ● | If toDate is not provided in the request then currentDate will be considered as toDate. |
| ● | The Fetched Records will be in Ascending Order. |
| ● | If no filter is provided in the request then, last 1 year data will fetched. |
| ● | The request parameters should be passed in the request params and not in the |

request body.

- **●** The result will be returned in the Pagination manner, where in without passing page
size and page index you’ll get the whole records based on the filter applied and **when the page**

size and idex is passed in the request then , that index records with sepecified size will be

returned.

Example:

_/_UTLROnlineRemitAPI/Remittance/transactions/search/?page.size=10&page.index=5&t

xNumber=5540261924&txNumber=5540232541&startDate=01012022&endDate=0405

2022

195

**URI:** /UTLROnlineRemitAPI/Remittance/transactions/search

**UNIR Request:** SearchTransactionRequest

Attribute Validation Description Type Rule

| partnerCode | Mandatory | This information is provided by UniTeller and it should be String(2 Characters) - 20 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Page | Optional | exactly This information is used to the same as provided. SearchPage |

track a request and response. The interaction ID received in

request will be returned in response.

**Status** Optional. Requested transaction status List<String>

**txNumber** Optional Transaction Number List<String>

**startDate** Optional The startDate should be in String(8 Digits)string form MMDDYYYY (i.e.

month, day, year) with no separator between year,

month, day.**endDate** Optional The endDate should be in String(8 Digits)

string form MMDDYYYY (i.e. month, day, year) with no

separator between year, month, day.

**UNIR Response**: SearchResponse

| Attribute | Description | Type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| responseCode responseMessage | Returns Response Code Returns Response Message | String String |

**Results** Contains the list of transaction List of ITransactionDto. Seedetails Appendix A

**Page** Contains the page data(page index SearchPageand records in the page)

**totalPages** Total number of pages String**totalRecords** Total Number of Records Fetched String

**resultSize** Records in single page String

/UTLROnlineRemitAPI/Remittance/transactions/search/?startDate=01012022&endDate=020 52022

### 8 Appendix ‘B’

_8.1_ User’s profile status

User can have different status; these statuses are mentioned below-

**⮚** _REGISTRATION NOT COMPLETE:_ Once a user is signed up with the UNIR system, his

status becomes REGISTRATION NOT COMPLETE. Users cannot login into the UNIR system with

this status.

**⮚** _REGISTRATION COMPLETE:_ After Signup, an authentication code is sent to the user’s

registered email. User has to provide that authentication code to verify his/her email address

and after a successful authentication process, User becomes REGISTRATION COMPLETE. Users

can login to the UNIR system with this status and will be able to use some functionalities of the

UNIR system.

**⮚** _APPROVED:_ After login, when a user completes his/her profile setup, he/she becomes

APPROVED and can add his/her Sending Methods and Send Money.

| ⮚ | ACTIVE: After doing the first transaction of Send Money, the user becomes Active. |
| --- | --- |
| ⮚ | SUSPENDED: During setup profile or after setup profile, if user is found with any |

suspicious activities, either UNIR system or Compliance Team of UNIR will SUSPEND the user. A

SUSPENDED user cannot login to the UNIR system.

8.2 Transaction Status Types

**Hold** - There is some hold on the transaction. To the User it should be displayed as **In Process.**

**Payable** - From Payable status, Transaction can be Cancelled or Paid on payer side.

**Cancel** - Either User or Payer cancelled the transaction.

**Paid** - This is one of the final statuses of the transaction. There is a reversal case where PAIDtransaction becomes CANCEL from Core System.

**Refund** - Transaction is cancelled on another accounting day/time. Therefore money needs tobe refunded to the user. This is one of the final statuses of the transaction.

**CANCELPENDING** - Transaction is in transmitted state (Means transaction is moved to thePayer side and it can be Either Paid or can get Cancel).

218

8.3 Flow of APIs

_8.3.1_ Signup, OAuth Login and Setup profile

This set of APIs is used to register the user, login the user without password and set up their profile. Here is the flow-

219

**Description:** Here is the description of above flow-

1. Required information for signup is collected at client side and including users Address. 2. Collected information is validated.
3. If validation of data is successful then the signup () API method is called, otherwise the user is asked to provide the valid data.
4. If the user is successfully registered with the UNIR system then, its user Profile Status becomes REGISTRATION COMPLETE.
_5._ After Setup the User’s Profile user Profile Status becomes APPROVED.
*Authentication of the user’s email can be skipped, if the client has some procedure to verify user’s email and then no authentication code will be sent to user’s registered email, step steps 5, 6 and 7 will be skipped and

user will become REGISTRATION COMPLETE.

220

signup () method flow

**Description:** Here is the description of above flow-

1. Once signup request is received at server, first it is validated. 2. If validation is success, then UNIR system checks if user is duplicate or not, otherwise UNIR
system returns response code with error description message.

221

3. If user already exists in the UNIR system, then UNIR system returns 50107007 Response code and no signup will be created.
4. If user is not duplicate, UNIR checks, that user is blacklisted or not? If found in blacklist, no signup is created and UNIR system returns 50107005.
**5.** Otherwise, Signup is created. If there is any unexpected issue, UNIR system may returnresponse code 59906002 and **59906005**. In this case we recommend retry and if problem persists,
one should inform Customer Support to get this issue resolved.

222

223

224

_8.3.2_ Add Sending Method

Here is the Add Sending method flow-

| Description: 1) | Information is collected at client and validation is done. Here is the flow of above diagram - |
| --- | --- |
| 2) 3) | If found valid input, addSendingMethod () API method is called. UNIR system returns response code 59908005 if user has already added max number of |

sending methods allowed. And sending method will not be added to user profile.

225

4) If user is allowed to add sending method, then UNIR system checks sending method type. 4.1) If Sending Method is Credit Card, then-
_4.2.1)_ UNIR system checks number of Credit Card can be added to user profile and if total numberof added Credit Cards + Credit Card being added is greater than max allowed Credit Card in one

profile, then UNIR system will return response code **59908005** and Credit Card will not be added_to user’s profile._

_4.2.2)_ If same Credit Card is already added then, system will return response codes **59908006**_and Credit Card will not be added to user’s profile._

4.2.3) Otherwise Credit Card will be added as Sending Method. 4.2) If Sending Method is Debit Card, then-

_4.3.1)_ UNIR system checks number of Debit Card can be added to user profile and if total numberof added Debit Cards + Debit Card being added is greater than max allowed Debit Card in one

profile, then UNIR system will return response code **59908005** and Debit Card will not be added to_user’s profile._

_4.3.2)_ If same Debit Card is already added then, system will return response codes**, 59908007**_and Debit Card will not be added to user’s profile._

4.3.3) Otherwise Debit Card will be added as Sending Method.

### 10 Appendix ‘D’

__ Beneficiary Rules

Beneficiary Rules are the rules to check fraud with beneficiaries. The basis of this check is how many users are sending money to the same beneficiary and how much amount is sent.

Note that the number of users allowed to send money to the same beneficiary and total allowed amount sent to the beneficiary are configurable at UNIR and may change in future. If required,

this information can be provided to the client at the time of integration.

238

__ Sending Methods Limit

There is a limit of adding Sending Methods in one user profile. For Example, a user can add up to three sending methods only; including all valid sending methods (Debit Card, Credit Card).

Also there is a limit of adding one type of sending methods, like if Credit Card addition limit is one then, user cannot add more than 1 Credit Card even if Sending methods limit is three. Note

that the number of Sending Methods allowed for a User are configurable at UNIR and may change in future.

239

### 11 Appendix ‘E’

11.1 Terminologies

11.2 Identification code

Identification code is a code which can be sent to user at various places during the use of UNIR application. Whenever it is required to send, UNIR system asks for receiving mode. These

receiving modes can be either registered email address or registered mobile phone (with capability of receiving text).

11.3 UNIR System

UNIR System is short form of UniTeller Internet Remittances System.

11.4 Information Verification

Information Verification is a process of verifying user’s provided data. This can be done without**asking for any other additional information.**

11.5 Sending Methods

Sending Methods are used by users to make payment at the time of transaction creation. These are Bank Account, Credit Card and Debit Card.

11.6 Payer

Payer is the institution from which the beneficiary will collect the amount. This institution can be a bank or other institutions.

11.7 Reception Method

The Reception Method is the mode of receiving the amount by the beneficiary. This can be either Cash PickUp, Account Credit/Home Delivery and WALLET

### 12 Appendix ‘F’

FAQ

**Q.** What is the minimum transaction amount to be sent by the user?

240

_A._ Min transaction amount is USD - 25$.

**Q.** What is the maximum number of beneficiaries that a user can add?

**A.** Right now a user can add up-to 6 beneficiaries and this number can be increased/decreased_after BD Team’s approval._

**Q.** How much time does it take to reflect the remitter’s address(Billing Address/Sending Method_Address) in the profile section?_

**A.** Instantly

**Q.** Are receipt’s attributes editable?

**A**. No, receipt is not editable as it is read only and static in nature.

**Q.** What beneficiary's attributes/fields can be edited?

**A.** An user cannot edit First name and last name, apart from these attributes, an user can editevery field.

**Q**. Who can cancel any transaction?

**A**. A transaction can be cancelled by the user itself and UniTeller Admin.

**Q**. Is there a possibility that the user can't cancel the transaction? If yes, how would they cancelthe transaction?

**A**. Yes, If the transaction is in "PAID" and “REFUND” Status. In such cases the user cannot cancelthe transaction by itself. But if the transaction is in “PAYABLE” and “IN PROCESS” status, the

user can cancel them.

**Q.** Is it possible to edit one cash pickup beneficiary to account credit beneficiary or vice versa?

_A._ No.

**Q**. Is additional field associated with every user/remitter?

**A**. No, Additional fields are related to the payer reception method. It varies from payer to payer,and not all-payers require additional fields, so an user/remitter is not associated with

additional fields rather associated with payer branches.

**Q**. Why is user profile setup required?

**A**. Without remitter profile setup userComplianceLimit API won't work and many other API'swill also not work.

**Q**. For addBeneficiary, there is an accType field that it says is needed if the user provides areception method of Account credit - what values are expected for it?

241

**A**. This field is required for Account Credit when in API PayerWithReceptionMethodsList Valueof isAddtionalFieldRequired is YES. The Value of AccType can be fetched using API

PayerAdditionalField.

**Q**. Will the exchange rate received from CurrentExchangeRate potentially change in valuedepending on the payer or reception method used, or will it be the same?

**A**. Exchange rate is not based on Payers or sending method or reception method. In general,Exchange rate is associated with the destination country and destination currency.

**Q**. Each user will need to call Signup to register themselves with UniTeller, After that they willneed to password authorize (email and currently deviceinfo - but device info will be optional)

before they can use any of the password credential endpoints?

**A**. Password Grant type is required for operation related to Remitter.

**Q**. Is it possible to update a beneficiary’s reception method using edit beneficiary?

**A**. API does not allow changing the Reception Method for a beneficiary using editBeneficiaryAPI. First Name, Last Name, Payment Currency and Payer (only if there is not any transaction in

process for that beneficiary) are not editable.

**Q**. Do all of those fields need to be passed back to UniTeller when adding additional fields for abeneficiary, or are only the fieldId and fieldValue needed at that point?

**A**. Yes, while calling you will need to pass the additional field details ( fieldName and fieldValue)in addBeneficiary API request.

**Q**. Is there a way to get all payers for a given country without having to provide the state?

**A**. No, we cannot get all payer of a given country without having to provide a state becausePayer is linked with the state because there are states in different countries that may need

payer branch information depending upon the selection of the state related to that specific county.

**Q**. When the value of isAddtionalFieldRequired will be YES in APIv2/PayerWithReceptionMethodsList?

**A**. The isAddtionalFieldRequired will be YES in API v2/PayerWithReceptionMethodsList if thereare additional Fields for a payer related to beneficiary or transaction.

**Q**. If we want to allow more beneficiaries, can the number be changed?

**A**. Yes, after approval from BD Team.

**Q**. Are there any format requirements for the beneficiary phone number?

**A**. There is Min/Max validation for Beneficiary Phone Number (1-20 Digits) and it should notinclude the country Code.

242

**Q**. What is credit level and compliance level?

**A**. Credit level is how much a remitter can send to all beneficiaries in total and it is set up by the_UniTeller’s business team and it varies based upon user level (Silver, Gold, Diamond,Platinum)_

and it is configurable. Compliance level is the source count_ry’s state wise limits that is specific to_that state and it is configured as day wise intervals(e.g. 4 days, 7 days, 30 days and so on) and is

provided by UniTeller’s compliance team.

**Q**. What expires_in field received in response to password and client_credential grant request?

**A**. This expires_in field is time duration until user session will be kept active/alive and it is_received along with access token(In other words it is time duration on this access_token). It’s_

configurable, which means it can be increased or decreased as per requirement.

**Q**. Is it necessary to call refresh_token API ?

**A**. Yes, it must be called to keep the user's session active/alive. It expires_in of refresh_token is(access_token expires_in + 10min).
